Show all your reasoning.\\nAt the end, put your final answer letter in \\\\\\\\boxed{{}}, e.g. \\\\\\\\boxed{{A}}.\\n\\n{problem}\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef extract_yesno_answer(text: str) -> str:\\n    text_lower = text.lower().strip()\\n    m = re.search(r'the answer is\\\\s*(yes|no)\\\\b', text_lower)\\n    if m:\\n        return m.group(1).capitalize()\\n    last_lines = text.strip().split('\\\\n')[-3:]\\n    for line in reversed(last_lines):\\n        ll = line.lower().strip()\\n        if 'yes' in ll and 'no' not in ll:\\n            return 'Yes'\\n        if 'no' in ll and 'yes' not in ll:\\n            return 'No'\\n    return \\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef extract_choice_answer(text: str) -> str:\\n    m = re.search(r'the answer is\\\\s*\\\\(?([A-D])\\\\)?', text, re.IGNORECASE)\\n    if m:\\n        return m.group(1).upper()\\n    boxed = extract_boxed_answer(text)\\n    if boxed and boxed.upper() in ('A', 'B', 'C', 'D'):\\n        return boxed.upper()\\n    return \\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef extract_boxed_answer(text: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract the last \\\\\\\\boxed{...} content from text.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    matches = re.findall(r'\\\\\\\\boxed\\\\{([^}]*(?:\\\\{[^}]*\\\\}[^}]*)*)\\\\}', text)\\n    return matches[-1].strip() if matches else \\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\nclass TraceGenerator(ABC):\\n    domain: str = \\\"math\\\"\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def generate(self, problem: str, k: int = 12) -> list[dict]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate K reasoning traces.          temperature: float = 0.7, top_p: float = 0.95,\\n                 max_tokens: int = 4096):\\n        import openai\\n        self.client = openai.OpenAI(base_url=base_url, api_key=[REDACTED] return {\\\"trace\\\": text, \\\"answer\\\": answer, \\\"trace_idx\\\": trace_idx}\\n\\n    def generate(self, problem: str, k: int = 12) -> list[dict]:\\n        prompt = self._build_prompt(problem)\\n\\n        async def _run():\\n            tasks = [self._generate_single(prompt, i) for i in range(k)]\\n            return await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\\n\\n  \\\"\\\"\\\"Mock generator for testing. Supports vLLM API, external API, and mock mode.\\nInput: math problem string, K (number of traces)\\nOutput: list of {\\\"trace\\\": str, \\\"answer\\\": str} dicts\\nDependencies: openai, re, asyncio\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport asyncio\\nimport re\\nimport logging\\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSOLVE_PROMPT = \\\"\\\"\\\"Solve the following math problem step by step. Show all your reasoning.\\nAt the end, put your final answer in \\\\\\\\boxed{{}}.\\n\\nProblem: {problem}\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\n\\nCOMMONSENSE_SOLVE_PROMPT = \\\"\\\"\\\"Answer the following Yes/No question step by step. Show all your reasoning.\\nAt the end, clearly state your final answer as \\\\\\\\boxed{Yes} or \\\\\\\\boxed{No}.\\n\\nQuestion: {problem}\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nMULTICHOICE_SOLVE_PROMPT = \\\"\\\"\\\"Answer the following multiple-choice question step by step. Show all your reasoning.\\nAt the end, put your final answer letter in \\\\\\\\boxed{{}}, e.g. \\\\\\\\boxed{{A}}.\\n\\n{problem}\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef extract_yesno_answer(text: str) -> str:\\n    text_lower = text.lower().strip()\\n    m = re.search(r'the answer is\\\\s*(yes|no)\\\\b', text_lower)\\n    if m:\\n        return m.group(1).capitalize()\\n    last_lines = text.strip().split('\\\\n')[-3:]\\n    for line in reversed(last_lines):\\n        ll = line.lower().strip()\\n        if 'yes' in ll and 'no' not in ll:\\n            return 'Yes'\\n        if 'no' in ll and 'yes' not in ll:\\n            return 'No'\\n    return \\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef extract_choice_answer(text: str) -> str:\\n    m = re.search(r'the answer is\\\\s*\\\\(?([A-D])\\\\)?', text, re.IGNORECASE)\\n    if m:\\n        return m.group(1).upper()\\n    boxed = extract_boxed_answer(text)\\n    if boxed and boxed.upper() in ('A', 'B', 'C', 'D'):\\n        return boxed.upper()\\n    return \\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef extract_boxed_answer(text: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract the last \\\\\\\\boxed{...} content from text.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    matches = re.findall(r'\\\\\\\\boxed\\\\{([^}]*(?:\\\\{[^}]*\\\\}[^}]*)*)\\\\}', text)\\n    return matches[-1].strip() if matches else \\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\nclass TraceGenerator(ABC):\\n    domain: str = \\\"math\\\"\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def generate(self, problem: str, k: int = 12) -> list[dict]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate K reasoning traces. Returns list of {\\\"trace\\\": str, \\\"answer\\\": str}.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        ...\\n\\n\\nclass VLLMGenerator(TraceGenerator):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"vLLM server inference via OpenAI-compatible API with async concurrency.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, base_url: str = \\\"http://localhost:8000/v1\\\",\\n                 api_key: [REDACTED] = \\\"EMPTY\\\", model: str | None = None,\\n                 temperature: float = 0.7, top_p: float = 0.95,\\n                 max_tokens: int = 4096):\\n        import openai\\n        self.client = openai.OpenAI(base_url=base_url, api_key=[REDACTED]        self.async_client = openai.AsyncOpenAI(base_url=base_url, api_key=[REDACTED]        self.temperature = temperature\\n        self.top_p = top_p\\n        self.max_tokens = max_tokens\\n        if model:\\n            self.model = model\\n        else:\\n            models = self.client.models.list()\\n            self.model = models.data[0].id\\n            logger.info(\\\"Auto-detected model: %s\\\", self.model)\\n\\n    def _build_prompt(self, problem: str) -> str:\\n        if self.domain == \\\"commonsense\\\":\\n            return COMMONSENSE_SOLVE_PROMPT.format(problem=problem)\\n        elif self.domain == \\\"multichoice\\\":\\n            return MULTICHOICE_SOLVE_PROMPT.format(problem=problem)\\n        return SOLVE_PROMPT.format(problem=problem)\\n\\n    async def _generate_single(self, prompt: str, trace_idx: int) -> dict:\\n        response = await self.async_client.chat.completions.create(\\n            model=self.model,\\n            messages=[{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": prompt}],\\n            temperature=self.temperature,\\n            top_p=self.top_p,\\n            max_tokens=self.max_tokens,\\n        )\\n        text = response.choices[0].message.content or \\\"\\\"\\n        answer = extract_boxed_answer(text)\\n        if not answer and self.domain == \\\"commonsense\\\":\\n            answer = extract_yesno_answer(text)\\n        elif not answer and self.domain == \\\"multichoice\\\":\\n            answer = extract_choice_answer(text)\\n        return {\\\"trace\\\": text, \\\"answer\\\": answer, \\\"trace_idx\\\": trace_idx}\\n\\n    def generate(self, problem: str, k: int = 12) -> list[dict]:\\n        prompt = self._build_prompt(problem)\\n\\n        async def _run():\\n            tasks = [self._generate_single(prompt, i) for i in range(k)]\\n            return await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\\n\\n        try:\\n            loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()\\n            import nest_asyncio\\n            nest_asyncio.apply()\\n            return loop.run_until_complete(_run())\\n        except RuntimeError:\\n            return asyncio.run(_run())\\n\\n\\nclass APIGenerator(TraceGenerator):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Generic OpenAI-compatible API inference.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self,       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nSICA end-to-end pipeline: trace generation -> constraint extraction -> Z3 dedup -> MAX-SAT -> scoring -> answer selection.\\nInput: problem dict with \\\"problem\\\" and \\\"answer\\\" fields\\nOutput: result dict with answer, score, constraint stats, maxsat stats\\nDependencies: trace_generator, constraint_extractor, z3_maxsat, scorer\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport logging\\nimport time\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nfrom .trace_generator import TraceGenerator\\nfrom .constraint_extractor import ConstraintExtractor\\nfrom .z3_maxsat import ConstraintDeduplicator, MaxSATSolver\\nfrom .scorer import InvariantScorer\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\nclass SICAPipeline:\\n    def __init__(\\n        self,\\n        trace_generator: TraceGenerator,\\n        constraint_extractor: ConstraintExtractor,\\n        k: int = 12,\\n        maxsat_timeout_ms: int = 10000,\\n    ):\\n        self.trace_generator = trace_generator\\n        self.constraint_extractor = constraint_extractor\\n        self.k = k\\n        self.deduplicator = ConstraintDeduplicator()\\n        self.solver = MaxSATSolver()\\n        self.scorer = InvariantScorer()\\n        self.maxsat_timeout_ms = maxsat_timeout_ms\\n\\n    def run_single(self, problem: dict) -> dict:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Run full SICA pipeline on a single problem.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        t_start = time.perf_counter()\\n\\n        # 1. Generate K traces\\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n        traces = self.trace_generator.generate(problem[\\\"problem\\\"], k=self.k)\\n        trace_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n\\n        # 2. Extract constraints from each trace (batch for concurrency)\\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n        all_constraints = self.constraint_extractor.extract_batch(\\n            [t[\\\"trace\\\"] for t in traces]\\n        )\\n        extract_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n\\n        total_constraints = sum(len(c) for c in all_constraints)\\n        non_empty = sum(1 for c in all_constraints if c)\\n\\n        # 3. Deduplicate via z3 equivalence\\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n        unique_constraints = self.deduplicator.deduplicate(all_constraints)\\n        dedup_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n\\n        # 4. MAX-SAT solve\\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n        maxsat_result = self.solver.solve(unique_constraints, timeout_ms=self.maxsat_timeout_ms)\\n        maxsat_time = time.perf_counter() - t0\\n\\n        # 5. Normalize logic answers before scoring\\n        if problem.get(\\\"dataset\\\", \\\"math\\\") in (\\\"proofwriter\\\", \\\"folio\\\", \\\"strategyqa\\\"):\\n            for t in traces:\\n                if t.get(\\\"answer\\\"):\\n                    t[\\\"answer\\\"] = normalize_logic_answer(t[\\\"answer\\\"])\\n\\n        # 6. <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have a good understanding of the code structure. Let me note the key points:

1. **`run_full_mvp.py`** is the main entry point. It:
   - Parses args including `--temperature` (single float, default 0.7) and `--k` (number of traces)
   - `build_generator(args)` creates a `VLLMGenerator` with a single temperature
   - `SICAPipeline` is created with the generator and extractor
   - For each problem, `pipeline.run_single(problem)` is called
   - Inside `run_single`, the trace generator produces k traces at that temperature, then constraint extraction, deduplication, MAX-SAT solving, and scoring all happen in sequence

2. **`VLLMGenerator`** in `trace_generator.py`:
   - Takes a single `temperature` in constructor
   - `generate(problem, k)` spawns k async tasks that each call the API with the same temperature
   - Returns list of `{"trace": str, "answer": str, "trace_idx": int}`

3. **`SICAPipeline.run_single`** orchestrates the full pipeline after traces are generated

, handling constraint extraction through final scoring and returning a result dict with all stats

For multi-temperature sampling, I need to generate traces in batches at different temperatures rather than all at once, merge them into a single list, and attach temperature metadata to each trace for later analysis. The downstream processing stays unchanged. I'm planning to create a new `run_multi_temp.py` script based on the existing MVP, where the main modification is replacing the single-temperature generator with logic that builds generators for each temperature and combines their outputs.

Since `VLLMGenerator` stores temperature as an instance attribute, I could either create separate generator instances per temperature or wrap the generation logic. The simpler approach is creating multiple generators. But looking at how `SICAPipeline.run_single` actually calls the trace generator internally, I need to decide whether to modify the pipeline itself to support multi-temperature generation or generate traces separately and feed them in.

traces across multiple temperatures. k is ignored; uses temp_k_pairs.\"\"\"\n        prompt = self._build_prompt(problem)\n\n        async def _run():\n            tasks = []\n            idx = 0\n            for temp, k_t in self.temp_k_pairs:\n                for _ in range(k_t):\n                    tasks.append(self._generate_single(prompt, idx, temp))\n                    idx += 1\n            return await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n\n        try:\n            loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()\n            import nest_asyncio\n            nest_asyncio.apply()\n            return list(loop.run_until_complete(_run()))\n        except RuntimeError:\n            return list(asyncio.run(_run()))\n\n\nclass MultiTempAPIGenerator(TraceGenerator):\n    \"\"\"Generates traces at multiple temperatures using generic OpenAI-compatible API (sequential).\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, base_url: str, api_key: [REDACTED], model: str,\n                 temp_k_pairs: list[tuple[float, int]],\n                 top_p: float = 0.95, max_tokens: int = 4096):\n        import openai\n        self.client = openai.OpenAI(base_url=base_url, api_key=[REDACTED]\n        self.model = model\n        self.top_p = top_p\n        self.max_tokens = max_tokens\n        self.temp_k_pairs = temp_k_pairs\n\n    def _build_prompt(self, problem: str) -> str:\n        if self.domain == \"commonsense\":\n            return COMMONSENSE_SOLVE_PROMPT.format(problem=problem)\n        elif self.domain == \"multichoice\":\n            return MULTICHOICE_SOLVE_PROMPT.format(problem=problem)\n        return SOLVE_PROMPT.format(problem=problem)\n\n    def generate(self, problem: str, k: int = 12) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Generate traces sequentially across multiple temperatures.\"\"\"\n        prompt = self._build_prompt(problem)\n        results = []\n        idx = 0\n        for temp, k_t in self.temp_k_pairs:\n            for _ in range(k_t):\n                resp = self.client.chat.completions.create(\n                    model=self.model,\n                    messages=[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": prompt}],\n                    temperature=temp,\n                    top_p=self.top_p,\n                    max_tokens=self.max_tokens,\n                )\n                text = resp.choices[0].message.content or \"\"\n                answer = extract_boxed_answer(text)\n                if not answer and self.domain == \"commonsense\":\n                    answer = extract_yesno_answer(text)\n                elif not answer and self.domain == \"multichoice\":\n                    answer = extract_choice_answer(text)\n                results.append({\"trace\": text, \"answer\": answer, \"trace_idx\": idx, \"temperature\": temp})\n                idx += 1\n        return results\n\n\ndef parse_temp_k_pairs(temperatures_str: str, k_per_temp_str: str) -> list[tuple[float, int]]:\n    \"\"\"Parse comma-separated temperature and k-per-temp strings into paired list.\"\"\"\n    temps = [float(t.strip()) for t in temperatures_str.split(\",\")]\n    ks = [int(k.strip()) for k in k_per_temp_str.split(\",\")]\n    if len(temps) != len(ks):\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"--temperatures has {len(temps)} values but --k-per-temp has {len(ks)}; they must match\"\n        )\n    return list(zip(temps, ks))\n\n\ndef build_multi_temp_generator(args, temp_k_pairs):\n    \"\"\"Build a multi-temperature trace generator based on mode.\"\"\"\n    if args.mode == \"mock\":\n        return MockGenerator()\n    elif args.mode == \"vllm\":\n        return MultiTempVLLMGenerator(\n            base_url=args.api_base,\n            api_key=[REDACTED],\n            model=args.model if args.model != \"auto\" else None,\n            temp_k_pairs=temp_k_pairs,\n            max_tokens=args.max_tokens,\n        )\n    elif args.mode == \"api\":\n        return MultiTempAPIGenerator(\n            base_url=args.api_base,\n            api_key=[REDACTED],\n            model=args.model,\n            temp_k_pairs=temp_k_pairs,\n            max_tokens=args.max_tokens,\n        )\n    raise ValueError(f\"Unknown mode: {args.mode}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"SICA Multi-Temperature Experiment\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--mode\", choices=[\"mock\", \"vllm\", \"api\"], default=\"vllm\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--temperatures\", type=str, default=\"0.3,0.7,1.2\",\n                        help=\"Comma-separated sampling temperatures (e.g. 0.3,0.7,1.2)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--k-per-temp\", type=str, default=\"4,4,4\", dest=\"k_per_temp\",\n                        help=\"Comma-separated trace counts per temperature (must match --temperatures length)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--model\", type=str, default=\"auto\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--api-base\", type=str, default=\"http://localhost:8000/v1\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--api-key\", type=str, default=\"EMPTY\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--math-data\", type=str, default=\"data/math500_subset.json\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--pw-data\", type=str, default=\"data/proofwriter_subset.json\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output\", type=str, default=\"results/multi_temp_results.json\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--resume\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Resume from checkpoint\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--max-tokens\", type=int, default=4096, dest=\"max_tokens\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=None)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--folio-data\", type=str, default=None, dest=\"folio_data\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--strategyqa-data\", type=str, default=None, dest=\"strategyqa_data\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--save-intermediates\", action=\"store_true\", dest=\"save_intermediates\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    temp_k_pairs = parse_temp_k_pairs(args.temperatures, args.k_per_temp)\n    total_k = sum(k for _, k in temp_k_pairs)\n\n    if args.seed is not None:\n        import random\n        random.seed(args.seed)\n\n    temp_desc = \" + \".join(f\"T={t}x{k}\" for t, k in temp_k_pairs)\n    logger.info(\"SICA Multi-Temp | mode=%s K=%d (%s) resume=%s\",\n                args.mode, total_k, temp_desc, args.resume)\n\n    problems = load_all_problems(args.math_data, args.pw_data,\n                                 folio_path=args.folio_data,\n                                 strategyqa_path=args.strategyqa_data)\n\n    completed_ids = set()\n    completed_results = []\n    if args.resume:\n        checkpoint = load_checkpoint(args.output)\n        if checkpoint and \"results\" in checkpoint:\n            completed_results = checkpoint[\"results\"]\n            completed_ids = {r[\"problem_id\"] for r in completed_results if \"problem_id\" in r}\n            logger.info(\"Resuming: %d problems already completed\", len(completed_ids))\n\n    generator = build_multi_temp_generator(args, temp_k_pairs)\n    extractor = build_extractor(args)\n    pipeline = SICAPipeline(trace_generator=generator, constraint_extractor=extractor, k=total_k)\n    sc_baseline = SelfConsistency()\n\n    all_results = list(completed_results)\n    elapsed_times = []\n    total_start = time.time()\n\n    remaining = [(i, p) for i, p in enumerate(problems) if p[\"id\"] not in completed_ids]\n    logger.info(\"Running %d remaining problems (skipped %d)\", len(remaining), len(completed_ids))\n\n    for progress_idx, (i, prob) in enumerate(remaining):\n        t0 = time.time()\n        prob_id = prob[\"id\"]\n        dataset = prob.get(\"dataset\", \"math\")\n        level = prob.get(\"level\", \"unknown\")\n\n        print(f\"\\n--- [{progress_idx+1}/{len(remaining)}] (overall {i+1}/{len(problems)}) ---\")\n        print(f\"ID: {prob_id} | Dataset: {dataset} | Level: {level}\")\n        print(f\"Q: {prob['problem'][:120]}...\")\n        print(f\"GT: {prob['answer']}\")\n        sys.stdout.flush()\n\n        if dataset in (\"proofwriter\", \"folio\"):\n            extractor.domain = \"logic\"\n            pipeline.trace_generator.domain = \"math\"\n        elif dataset == \"strategyqa\":\n            extractor.domain = \"commonsense\"\n            pipeline.trace_generator.domain = \"commonsense\"\n        elif dataset == \"logiqa\":\n            extractor.domain = \"logic\"\n            pipeline.trace_generator.domain = \"multichoice\"\n        else:\n            extractor.domain = \"math\"\n            pipeline.trace_generator.domain = \"math\"\n\n        try:\n            sica_result = pipeline.run_single(prob)\n            sica_answer = sica_result.get(\"answer\", \"\")\n            sica_correct = is_equiv(sica_answer, prob[\"answer\"])\n\n            cs = sica_result.get(\"constraints_stats\", {})\n            ms = sica_result.get(\"maxsat_stats\", {})\n            timing = sica_result.get(\"timing\", {})\n\n            print(f\"SICA: {sica_answer} ({'V' if sica_correct else 'X'})\")\n            print(f\"  Constraints: {cs.get('total_extracted',0)} extracted -> {cs.get('unique_after_dedup',0)} unique\")\n            print(f\"  MaxSAT: {ms.get('satisfied',0)} sat, {ms.get('excluded',0)} excl, solve={ms.get('solve_time_ms',0)}ms\")\n            print(f\"  Timing: trace={timing.get('trace_gen_s',0):.1f}s extract={timing.get('extraction_s',0):.1f}s total={timing.get('total_s',0):.1f}s\")\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.error(\"SICA error on %s: %s\", prob_id, e)\n            import traceback; traceback.print_exc()\n            sica_result = {\"answer\": \"\", \"traces\": [], \"scores\": {},\n                           \"constraints_stats\": {\"total_extracted\": 0, \"traces_with_constraints\": 0, \"unique_after_dedup\": 0},\n                           \"maxsat_stats\": {\"satisfied\": 0, \"excluded\": 0, \"total_weight\": 0, \"solve_time_ms\": 0},\n                           \"timing\": {}}\n            sica_answer = \"\"\n            sica_correct = False\n\n        try:\n            traces = sica_result.get(\"traces\", [])\n            answers = [t.get(\"answer\", \"\") for t in traces]\n            sc_result = sc_baseline.run(prob, traces=[t.get(\"trace\",\"\") for t in traces], answers=answers)\n            sc_answer = sc_result.get(\"answer\", \"\")\n            sc_correct = is_equiv(sc_answer, prob[\"answer\"])\n            print(f\"SC:   {sc_answer} ({'V' if sc_correct else 'X'}) -- votes: {sc_result.get('vote_count','?')}/{len(traces)}\")\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.error(\"SC error on %s: %s\", prob_id, e)\n            sc_answer = \"\"\n            sc_correct = False\n            sc_result = {\"vote_count\": 0, \"vote_distribution\": {}}\n\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        elapsed_times.append(elapsed)\n        print(f\"Time: {elapsed:.1f}s\")\n\n        avg_time = sum(elapsed_times) / len(elapsed_times)\n        remaining_count = len(remaining) - (progress_idx + 1)\n        eta_s = avg_time * remaining_count\n        if remaining_count > 0:\n            eta_min = eta_s / 60\n            print(f\"ETA: {eta_min:.1f} min ({remaining_count} remaining, avg {avg_time:.1f}s/prob)\")\n\n        sys.stdout.flush()\n\n        result_entry = {\n            \"problem_idx\": i,\n            \"problem_id\": prob_id,\n            \"problem\": prob[\"problem\"],\n            \"dataset\": dataset,\n            \"level\": level,\n            \"ground_truth\": prob[\"answer\"],\n            \"sica_answer\": sica_answer,\n            \"sica_scores\": sica_result.get(\"scores\", {}),\n            \"sica_correct\": sica_correct,\n            \"sc_answer\": sc_answer,\n            \"sc_vote_count\": sc_result.get(\"vote_count\", 0),\n            \"sc_vote_distribution\": sc_result.get(\"vote_distribution\", {}),\n            \"sc_correct\": sc_correct,\n            \"constraints_stats\": sica_result.get(\"constraints_stats\", {}),\n            \"maxsat_stats\": sica_result.get(\"maxsat_stats\", {}),\n            \"timing\": sica_result.get(\"timing\", {}),\n            \"wall_time_s\": round(elapsed, 2),\n        }\n        all_results.append(result_entry)\n\n        if args.save_intermediates:\n            intermed_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(args.output) or \".\", \"intermediates\")\n            os.makedirs(intermed_dir, exist_ok=True)\n            intermed_data = {\n                \"problem\": prob,\n                \"sica_result\": sica_result,\n            }\n            with open(os.path.join(intermed_dir, f\"{prob_id}.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n                json.dump(intermed_data, f, indent=2, default=str)\n\n            ptc_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(args.output) or \".\", \"per_trace_constraints\")\n            os.makedirs(ptc_dir, exist_ok=True)\n            traces = sica_result.get(\"traces\", [])\n            ptc_list = sica_result.get(\"per_trace_constraints\", [])\n            per_trace_out = []\n            for t_idx, t in enumerate(traces):\n                per_trace_out.append({\n                    \"trace_idx\": t.get(\"trace_idx\", t_idx),\n                    \"answer\": t.get(\"answer\", \"\"),\n                    \"temperature\": t.get(\"temperature\"),\n                    \"constraints\": ptc_list[t_idx] if t_idx < len(ptc_list) else [],\n                })\n            ptc_data = {\"pid\": prob_id, \"gt\": prob.get(\"answer\", \"\"), \"per_trace\": per_trace_out}\n            with open(os.path.join(ptc_dir, f\"{prob_id}.json\"), \"w\") as f:\n                json.dump(ptc_data, f, indent=2, default=str)\n\n        summary = compute_summary(all_results, extractor.stats)\n        summary[\"k\"] = total_k\n        summary[\"mode\"] = args.mode\n        summary[\"temperatures\"] = args.temperatures\n        summary[\"k_per_temp\"] = args.k_per_temp\n        summary[\"total_wall_time_s\"] = round(time.time() - total_start, 1)\n        output = {\"summary\": summary, \"results\": all_results}\n        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.output) or \".\", exist_ok=True)\n        with open(args.output, \"w\") as f:\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2, default=str)\n\n    total_wall = time.time() - total_start\n    summary = compute_summary(all_results, extractor.stats)\n    summary[\"k\"] = total_k\n    summary[\"mode\"] = args.mode\n    summary[\"temperatures\"] = args.temperatures\n    summary[\"k_per_temp\"] = args.k_per_temp\n    summary[\"total_wall_time_s\"] = round(total_wall, 1)\n\n    output = {\"summary\": summary, \"results\": all_results}\n    with open(args.output, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2, default=str)\n\n    print_summary(summary)\n    temp_desc = \" + \".join(f\"T={t}x{k}\" for t, k in temp_k_pairs)\n    print(f\"\\nTemperature config: {temp_desc}\")\n    print(f\"Total wall time: {total_wall/60:.1f} min\")\n    print(f\"Results saved to {args.output}\")\n    print(\"MULTI_TEMP_DONE\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYEOF"

### 产出总结

**创建文件**: `/root/symb_invariant_consensus/run_multi_temp.py`

**新增参数**（替代原 `--temperature` 和 `--k`）:
| 参数 | 类型 | 默认值 | 说明 |
|------|------|--------|------|
| `--temperatures` | str | `0.3,0.7,1.2` | 逗号分隔温度列表 |
| `--k-per-temp` | str | `4,4,4` | 每个温度的 trace 数量，需与 temperatures 长度匹配 |

其余参数（`--mode`, `--model`, `--api-base`, `--output`, `--resume`, `--save-intermediates` 等）与 `run_full_mvp.py` 一致。

**新增类/函数**:
- `MultiTempVLLMGenerator` — 单 async client，所有温度组并发生成，每条 trace 附带 `temperature` 字段
- `MultiTempAPIGenerator` — 顺序生成的 API 版本
- `parse_temp_k_pairs()` — 解析并校验温度/k 配置
- `build_multi_temp_generator()` — 工厂函数

**复用自 `run_full_mvp.py`**:
- `load_all_problems`, `load_checkpoint`, `build_extractor`, `compute_summary`, `print_summary`

**温度元数据记录位置**:
- 每条 trace dict 的 `"temperature"` 字段（vllm/api 模式）
- `per_trace_constraints/{id}.json` 中每条 trace 的 `"temperature"` 字段
- `summary` 中的 `"temperatures"` 和 `"k_per_temp"` 字段

**Dry-run 结果**: `--help` 解析正确，mock 模式完整 pipeline 跑通（2 题，12 traces = 4+4+4），参数校验（长度不匹配报错、单温度、不等分配）全部通过。
